WIN32API.INC

Source: rapidq_ref/incOld/WIN32API.INC, merged into rapidq_ref/inc_sys/rapidqAllProc.inc · 1337 functions · 1337 DLL functions

DLL functions are declared with DECLARE ... LIB; the name after ALIAS is the exported name. The description follows the Microsoft documentation; the link opens the search for that name on learn.microsoft.com. Windows API reference: https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows/win32/api/.
